874 entries, cairn-release to campaign-plan. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cairn-release**: Prepare a release following the active toolchain profile's release-walk - an r-package profile runs the CRAN walk (version bump, NEWS, checks, cran-comments, human submission checklist); a generic profile does a version bump + changelog + tag. Use when the user wants to release, submit to CRAN, cut a version, or prepare a release. Never self-submits. ([jmgirard/cairn](https://github.com/jmgirard/cairn/tree/HEAD/skills/cairn-release) · ★4)

([foobarto/cairn](https://github.com/foobarto/cairn/tree/HEAD/skills/cairn-review-phase) · ★1)
